`Schemas.ts`의 `army_garrison` 뒤(이 순서 = `DEPTH_ORDER_KINDS`):

```ts
const WwArmy = WwId;  // P2 army ids
const DesignSlot = zb.uint({ min: 1, max: WW_SHIP_DESIGN_SLOTS });
const DepthTarget = MappedID;  // a country (smallID)
const Region = zb.uint({ min: 1, max: SEA_ZONE_BASE - 1 });
const Zone = zb.uint({ max: 255 });  // strategic region index (P4 srIdx)
// …
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("doctrine_pick"), domain: z.enum(DOCTRINE_DOMAINS), id: z.enum(GRAND_DOCTRINES) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("subdoctrine_pick"), track: z.enum(DOCTRINE_TRACKS), id: z.enum(SUBDOCTRINES) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("army_spirit_pick"), category: z.enum(ARMY_SPIRIT_CATEGORIES), id: z.enum(ARMY_SPIRITS) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("trait_assign"), general: WwId, trait: z.union([z.enum(GENERAL_TRAITS), z.enum(FM_TRAITS), z.enum(ADMIRAL_TRAITS)]), admiral: z.boolean() }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("promote_fm"), general: WwId, trait: z.enum(FM_TRAITS) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("ag_create"), fm: WwId, armies: z.array(WwArmy).min(1).max(WW_GROUP_ARMIES) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("ag_add"), group: WwId, army: WwArmy }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("ag_remove"), group: WwId, army: WwArmy }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("ag_disband"), group: WwId }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("ag_front"), group: WwId, frontRegions: z.array(Region).min(1).max(120), enemy: zb.uint({ max: 255 }) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("ag_execute"), group: WwId }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("ag_auto"), group: WwId, on: z.boolean() }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("medal_award"), army: WwArmy, medal: z.enum(MEDALS) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("army_priority"), army: WwArmy, value: zb.uint({ max: 2 }) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("design_save"), slot: DesignSlot.optional(), def: DesignDefSchema }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("design_copy"), from: DesignSlot, def: DesignDefSchema }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("design_obsolete"), design: z.enum(DESIGN_KINDS), slot: DesignSlot }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("design_rename"), design: z.enum(DESIGN_KINDS), slot: DesignSlot, name: DesignNameSchema }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("ship_refit"), ship: WwId, slot: DesignSlot }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("line_convert"), line: WwId, from: zb.uint({ max: 4095 }).nullable() }),  // EQUIP_KEYS index; null: stop
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("mio_pick"), family: z.enum(MIO_FAMILIES), archetype: z.enum(MIO_ARCHETYPES) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("mio_trait"), family: z.enum(MIO_FAMILIES), trait: z.enum(MIO_TRAITS) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("mio_invest"), family: z.enum(MIO_FAMILIES) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("licence_request"), from: DepthTarget, key: zb.uint({ max: 4095 }), ship: DesignSlot.optional() }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("licence_answer"), request: WwId, accept: z.boolean() }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("licence_cancel"), licence: WwId }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("market_list"), key: zb.uint({ max: 4095 }), count: zb.uint({ min: 1, max: 1_000_000 }) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("market_unlist"), listing: WwId }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("market_buy"), listing: WwId, count: zb.uint({ min: 1, max: 1_000_000 }) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("embargo"), target: DepthTarget }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("embargo_lift"), target: DepthTarget }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("intel_found") }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("intel_upgrade"), id: z.enum(AGENCY_UPGRADES) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("operative_recruit") }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("operative_assign"), operative: WwId, target: DepthTarget, mission: z.enum(INTEL_MISSIONS) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("operative_recall"), operative: WwId }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("operation_start"), op: z.enum(INTEL_OPERATIONS), target: DepthTarget, operatives: z.array(WwId).min(1).max(2) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("operation_cancel"), operation: WwId }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("crypto_target"), target: DepthTarget }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("crypto_activate"), target: DepthTarget }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("project_start"), project: z.enum(SPECIAL_PROJECTS), facility: Region }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("project_cancel"), project: z.enum(SPECIAL_PROJECTS) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("project_scientist"), project: z.enum(SPECIAL_PROJECTS), scientist: zb.uint({ max: 15 }).nullable() }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("nuke_drop"), region: Region, rocket: z.boolean() }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("rocket_strike"), site: Region, target: Region, rocket: z.enum(ROCKET_KINDS), count: zb.uint({ min: 1, max: 30 }) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("raid"), raid: z.enum(RAID_KINDS), unit: WwId, target: Region, building: z.enum(BUILDING_IDS) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("raid_cancel"), raid: WwId }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("cfocus_set"), id: z.enum(CONTINUOUS_FOCUSES) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("cfocus_clear") }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("faction_rule"), rule: z.enum(FACTION_RULES), on: z.boolean() }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("faction_goal"), slot: zb.uint({ max: 2 }), goal: z.enum(FACTION_GOALS) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("treaty_leave") }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("railgun_assign"), army: WwArmy, count: zb.uint({ min: 1, max: WW_MAX_RAILGUNS }) }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("railgun_return"), army: WwArmy }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("harbor_deploy"), region: Region }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("scorch"), region: Region }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("air_night"), deployment: WwId, on: z.boolean() }),
```

- `design_save`의 `slot`이 없으면 그 종류의 첫 빈 칸. `ag_create`의 `armies`는 엔진이 정렬·중복 제거한다. `raid`의 `unit`은 사단 번호(공수·특공) 또는 배치 번호(정밀 폭격). `rocket_strike`의 `rocket`은 `ROCKET_KINDS`. `raid.building`은 목표 건물 종류(`BUILDING_IDS`, 엔진이 `RAID_TARGETS`로 거른다).
- `GameUpdates.ts`: `GameUpdateType.WwDepth`(맨 뒤, `WwEndgame` 다음), `WwDepthUpdate {type, data: WwDepthData}`, 합집합에 더함. `FrameNormalizer`(결정 36).
- `tests/zbin/wire.test.ts`: 57종마다 한 예씩 돌려 읽기(설계 정의 셋 포함), 이름 64자·`<` 든 이름 거절.

- [ ] **Step 5: 설계 규칙 `packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/DesignRules.ts`** (spec §5, 결정 6·10·11 — 전차 계산·역할·비용·검사·경험치·전환은 **기반 본문**, 함선·항공기 수치 함수도 기반 본문이고 E는 P4 변환과 게임 쪽만)

계산 규칙(spec §5.2.2 "계산 순서"를 정수로): ① 더하기(바탕 + 모듈, centi) → ② 모듈 %의 **합**(더하기와 깎기를 한 합으로, 바닥 −90 — spec의 "P2 `combineMods`"는 이 바닥을 뜻한다)을 곱하고 내림 → ③ 단계 %(장갑·엔진 단계의 합)를 곱하고 내림. 신뢰도는 ‰로 더하고 400~990으로 자른다. 비용은 더하기 → 모듈 비용 % → 단계 비용 %. 이 순서로 spec의 확인 줄이 정확히 나온다: 경 I 추천 = 연성 1200, 경성 400, 방어 400, 돌파 2160, 관통 1500, 장갑 900, 속도 800(centi); 중형 II 추천(장포신·3인 포탑·토션 바·용접·디젤·무선기·경사 장갑, 3/3) = 연성 1680, 경성 1800, 돌파 4130, 관통 6200, 방어 600, 장갑 5428, 속도 722, 신뢰도 875‰, 비용 19,550 milli; 중형 I 추천(단포신, 2/2, 무선기·연막) = 연성 2100, 경성 1000, 관통 3000, 돌파 3776.

```ts
import {
  DesignDef, DS_AMPHIBIOUS, DS_CARGO, DS_CARRIER, DS_FLAIL, DS_FLAME, DS_JET, DS_MINE_LAYER, DS_MINESWEEP,
  DS_RADAR, DS_RECON, DS_SKIRTS, DS_TD_DEFEND, DesignKind, PlaneDesignStats, ShipDesignMods,
  SPECIAL_PROJECTS, SpecialProject, TankDesignStats, TankRole,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/DepthTypes";
import { EQUIPMENT_TYPES, RESOURCE_IDS, ResourceId, WwOrderBlock, WwTechId } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/EconomyTypes";
import { NG_HEAVY, NG_SPA, NG_SPAA, NG_TD } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/LandTypes";
import { SHIP_CLASSES, SHIP_GENS, ShipClass } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/NavyTypes";
import { WW_TECHS } from "./WwTechs";

export interface DesignCtx {
  techs: ReadonlySet<WwTechId>;
  /** Bit i: SPECIAL_PROJECTS[i] done. */
  projects: number;
  /** MIO reliability for this design's family, percentage points (plan decision 11). */
  relPp: number;
  /** MIO cost for this design's family, percent. */
  costPct: number;
}
export interface DesignBlockCtx { techs: ReadonlySet<WwTechId>; projects: number; treatyMember: boolean; treatyEscalated: boolean }
const projectBit = (p: SpecialProject) => 1 << SPECIAL_PROJECTS.indexOf(p);
const res = (r: Partial<Record<ResourceId, number>>) => RESOURCE_IDS.map((id) => r[id] ?? 0);
const clamp = (v: number, lo: number, hi: number) => Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, v));
const mul = (v: number, pct: number) => Math.floor((v * (100 + Math.max(-90, pct))) / 100);

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- tanks (spec §5.2)
type Weight = "light" | "medium" | "heavy";
interface TankBaseDef {
  tech: WwTechId; weight: Weight; soft: number; hard: number; def: number; brk: number; armor: number;
  hardnessPm: number; speedCenti: number; relPm: number; costMilli: number; res: number[]; specials: 1 | 2;
}
const tb = (tech: WwTechId, weight: Weight, s: number, h: number, d: number, b: number, a: number,
  hard: number, sp: number, rel: number, cost: number, r: Partial<Record<ResourceId, number>>, specials: 1 | 2): TankBaseDef =>
  ({ tech, weight, soft: s * 100, hard: h * 100, def: d * 100, brk: b * 100, armor: a * 100, hardnessPm: hard * 10,
     speedCenti: sp * 100, relPm: rel * 10, costMilli: cost * 1000, res: res(r), specials });
export const TANK_BASE_DEFS: Readonly<Record<string, TankBaseDef>> = {
  light_1: tb("light_tank_1", "light", 4, 1, 4, 18, 10, 80, 8, 85, 7.5, { steel: 1, oil: 1 }, 1),
  light_2: tb("light_tank_2", "light", 4, 1, 4, 20, 14, 80, 9, 85, 8, { steel: 1, oil: 1 }, 2),
  medium_1: tb("medium_tank_1", "medium", 6, 4, 5, 24, 32, 90, 6, 80, 11, { steel: 2, oil: 1 }, 2),
  medium_2: tb("medium_tank_2", "medium", 6, 4, 6, 27, 40, 90, 6.5, 82, 12, { steel: 2, oil: 1 }, 2),
  medium_3: tb("medium_tank_3", "medium", 7, 5, 7, 30, 48, 92, 7, 85, 13, { steel: 2, oil: 1 }, 2),
  heavy_1: tb("heavy_tank_1", "heavy", 8, 5, 9, 24, 60, 95, 4, 70, 16, { steel: 3, chromium: 1, oil: 1 }, 2),
  heavy_2: tb("heavy_tank_2", "heavy", 8, 5, 10, 28, 80, 95, 4.5, 72, 18, { steel: 3, chromium: 1, oil: 1 }, 2),
  heavy_3: tb("heavy_tank_3", "heavy", 9, 6, 11, 32, 100, 96, 5, 75, 20, { steel: 3, chromium: 1, oil: 1 }, 2),
};
/** Slot kinds of a chassis: gun, turret, suspension, armor, engine, special × (1 or 2). */
export function tankSlots(base: string): string[] {
  const b = TANK_BASE_DEFS[base];
  return ["gun", "turret", "suspension", "armor", "engine", ...(b?.specials === 1 ? ["special"] : ["special", "special"])];
}
/** "" in a non-special slot means this module (spec §5.2.2 "기본"). */
export function tankDefault(base: string, slot: number): string {
  const w = TANK_BASE_DEFS[base]?.weight;
  return [w === "light" ? "auto20" : "gun_cs", base === "light_1" ? "turret_1" : "turret_2", "bogie",
    base === "light_1" ? "riveted" : "welded", "petrol"][slot] ?? "";
}
interface TankModDef {
  slot: string; tech?: WwTechId; weights?: readonly Weight[]; bases?: readonly string[];
  add?: { soft?: number; hard?: number; pierce?: number; brk?: number; def?: number; aa?: number };
  pct?: { soft?: number; hard?: number; brk?: number; def?: number; armor?: number; speed?: number };
  relPp?: number; fuelPct?: number; org?: number; costMilli?: number; costPct?: number;
  extra?: Partial<Record<ResourceId, number>>; flags?: number;
}
const ALL: readonly Weight[] = ["light", "medium", "heavy"];
const MH: readonly Weight[] = ["medium", "heavy"];
const LM: readonly Weight[] = ["light", "medium"];
export const TANK_MODULE_DEFS: Readonly<Record<string, TankModDef>> = {
  mg: { slot: "gun", add: { soft: 6, hard: 1, pierce: 5, brk: 2 }, costMilli: 300 },
  auto20: { slot: "gun", add: { soft: 8, hard: 3, pierce: 15, brk: 6 }, costMilli: 500 },
  gun_small: { slot: "gun", tech: "anti_tank_1", add: { soft: 6, hard: 8, pierce: 35, brk: 6 }, costMilli: 1_000 },
  gun_cs: { slot: "gun", tech: "artillery_1", weights: MH, add: { soft: 14, hard: 6, pierce: 30, brk: 8 }, costMilli: 1_500 },
  gun_hv: { slot: "gun", tech: "tank_gun_2", bases: ["light_2", "medium_1", "medium_2", "medium_3", "heavy_1", "heavy_2", "heavy_3"],
    add: { soft: 10, hard: 14, pierce: 62, brk: 8 }, costMilli: 2_500, extra: { tungsten: 1 } },
  gun_heavy: { slot: "gun", tech: "tank_gun_3", bases: ["medium_2", "medium_3", "heavy_1", "heavy_2", "heavy_3"],
    add: { soft: 12, hard: 20, pierce: 92, brk: 8 }, costMilli: 4_000, extra: { tungsten: 1 } },
  flame: { slot: "gun", tech: "flame_tank", add: { soft: 14, pierce: 2, brk: 10 }, costMilli: 1_200, flags: DS_FLAME },
  howitzer: { slot: "gun", tech: "artillery_2", add: { soft: 24, hard: 3, pierce: 15, def: 2 }, costMilli: 3_000 },
  aa_auto: { slot: "gun", tech: "anti_air_1", add: { soft: 3, hard: 3, pierce: 15, aa: 18 }, costMilli: 1_500 },
  turret_none: { slot: "turret", pct: { hard: 15, brk: -30 }, costPct: -15 },
  turret_open: { slot: "turret", pct: { soft: 5, armor: -20 }, costPct: -10 },
  turret_1: { slot: "turret", pct: { brk: -10 } },
  turret_2: { slot: "turret", bases: ["light_2", "medium_1", "medium_2", "medium_3", "heavy_1", "heavy_2", "heavy_3"], costMilli: 300 },
  turret_3: { slot: "turret", weights: MH, pct: { soft: 5, brk: 10 }, costMilli: 600 },
  bogie: { slot: "suspension" },
  christie: { slot: "suspension", weights: LM, pct: { speed: 15 }, relPp: -5, costMilli: 300 },
  torsion: { slot: "suspension", tech: "torsion_bar", pct: { speed: 5 }, relPp: 5, costMilli: 500 },
  interleaved: { slot: "suspension", tech: "interleaved_wheels", weights: MH, pct: { armor: 5, speed: 5 }, relPp: -10, costMilli: 800 },
  riveted: { slot: "armor", pct: { armor: -10 }, costMilli: -400 },
  welded: { slot: "armor" },
  cast: { slot: "armor", tech: "cast_armor", pct: { armor: 10 }, costMilli: 800, extra: { chromium: 1 } },
  petrol: { slot: "engine" },
  diesel: { slot: "engine", tech: "diesel_engine", relPp: 5, fuelPct: -25, costMilli: 300 },
  radio: { slot: "special", tech: "tank_radio", pct: { brk: 8 }, org: 2, costMilli: 300 },
  sloped: { slot: "special", tech: "sloped_armor", pct: { armor: 15 }, costMilli: 800 },
  aux_mg: { slot: "special", add: { soft: 2 }, costMilli: 200 },
  smoke: { slot: "special", pct: { def: 10 }, costMilli: 200 },
  skirts: { slot: "special", tech: "sloped_armor", pct: { speed: -3 }, costMilli: 300, flags: DS_SKIRTS },
  amphibious: { slot: "special", tech: "amphibious_drive", pct: { speed: -10 }, costMilli: 1_000, flags: DS_AMPHIBIOUS },
  flail: { slot: "special", tech: "engineer_company", pct: { speed: -10 }, costMilli: 500, flags: DS_FLAIL },
};
/** Modules with "" replaced by the chassis defaults (specials stay ""). */
export function resolvedModules(def: DesignDef): string[] {
  if (def.kind !== "tank") return [...def.modules];
  return def.modules.map((m, i) => (m === "" ? tankDefault(def.base, i) : m));
}
export function tankRole(def: DesignDef): TankRole {
  const m = resolvedModules(def);
  if (m[0] === "howitzer") return "sp_artillery";
  if (m[0] === "aa_auto") return "sp_anti_air";
  if (m[1] === "turret_none" && (m[0] === "gun_small" || m[0] === "gun_hv" || m[0] === "gun_heavy")) return "tank_destroyer";
  return "tank";
}
/** Plan decision 6. */
export function designNeedGroup(def: DesignDef): number {
  const role = tankRole(def);
  if (role === "tank_destroyer") return NG_TD;
  if (role === "sp_artillery") return NG_SPA;
  if (role === "sp_anti_air") return NG_SPAA;
  const w = TANK_BASE_DEFS[def.base].weight;
  return w === "light" ? EQUIPMENT_TYPES.indexOf("light_tank") : w === "medium" ? EQUIPMENT_TYPES.indexOf("medium_tank") : NG_HEAVY;
}
export function tankStats(def: DesignDef, ctx: DesignCtx): TankDesignStats {
  const b = TANK_BASE_DEFS[def.base];
  const add = { soft: b.soft, hard: b.hard, pierce: 0, brk: b.brk, def: b.def, aa: 0 };
  const pct = { soft: 0, hard: 0, brk: 0, def: 0, armor: 0, speed: 0, pierce: 0 };
  let rel = b.relPm + ctx.relPp * 10, fuel = 0, org = 0, cost = b.costMilli, costPct = 0, flags = 0;
  const r = [...b.res];
  for (const id of resolvedModules(def)) {
    if (id === "") continue;
    const m = TANK_MODULE_DEFS[id];
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(m.add ?? {})) add[k as keyof typeof add] += v * 100;
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(m.pct ?? {})) pct[k as keyof typeof pct] += v;
    rel += (m.relPp ?? 0) * 10; fuel += m.fuelPct ?? 0; org += m.org ?? 0;
    cost += m.costMilli ?? 0; costPct += m.costPct ?? 0; flags |= m.flags ?? 0;
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(m.extra ?? {})) r[RESOURCE_IDS.indexOf(k as ResourceId)] += v;
  }
  const role = tankRole(def);
  if (role === "tank_destroyer") { pct.hard += 20; pct.pierce += 10; flags |= DS_TD_DEFEND; }
  const aL = def.armorLevel ?? 0, eL = def.engineLevel ?? 0;
  const armorLvl = 6 * aL, speedLvl = -2 * aL + 4 * eL, costLvl = 3 * aL + 2 * eL, relLvl = -5 * aL - 10 * eL;
  return {
    kind: "tank", role, group: designNeedGroup(def),
    soft: mul(add.soft, pct.soft), hard: mul(add.hard, pct.hard), def: mul(add.def, pct.def), brk: mul(add.brk, pct.brk),
    pierce: mul(add.pierce, pct.pierce), aa: add.aa,
    armor: mul(mul(b.armor, pct.armor), armorLvl) + ((flags & DS_SKIRTS) !== 0 ? 400 : 0), // P2 reads armor only against pierce
    hardnessPm: b.hardnessPm,
    speedCenti: mul(mul(b.speedCenti, pct.speed), speedLvl),
    relPm: clamp(rel + relLvl, 400, 990),
    orgAdd: org, fuelPct: fuel + 5 * eL,
    costMilli: mul(mul(mul(cost, costPct), costLvl), ctx.costPct),
    resources: r, flags, year: WW_TECHS[b.tech].year,
  };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- ships (spec §5.3)
export const SHIP_CLASS_SLOTS: Readonly<Record<ShipClass, readonly string[]>> = {
  dd: ["torpedo", "asw", "secondary", "special"],
  cl: ["main", "secondary", "torpedo", "fire_control", "special"],
  ca: ["main", "secondary", "torpedo", "fire_control", "armor", "engine"],
  bc: ["main", "secondary", "fire_control", "armor", "engine", "special"],
  bb: ["main", "secondary", "fire_control", "armor", "engine", "special"],
  cv: ["hangar", "secondary", "armor", "fire_control", "engine"],
  ss: ["torpedo", "engine", "special"],
};
interface ShipModDef {
  slot: string; classes: readonly ShipClass[]; tech?: WwTechId; project?: SpecialProject; gens?: readonly number[];
  /** "main" scales light for a CL, heavy for CA/BC/BB (plan: the CL main battery is light). */
  m: Partial<Omit<ShipDesignMods, "kind" | "flags" | "year" | "costPct">> & { mainPct?: number };
  costPct: number; flags?: number;
}
const CAP: readonly ShipClass[] = ["cl", "ca", "bc", "bb"];
export const SHIP_MODULE_DEFS: Readonly<Record<string, ShipModDef>> = {
  main_heavy: { slot: "main", classes: CAP, m: { mainPct: 20, piercePct: 10, speedCentiKn: -100 }, costPct: 10 },
  main_light: { slot: "main", classes: CAP, m: { mainPct: -20, speedCentiKn: 100 }, costPct: -10 },
  sec_dp: { slot: "secondary", classes: ["cl", "ca", "bc", "bb", "cv", "dd"], tech: "dual_purpose_guns", m: { aaPct: 40, lightPct: 10 }, costPct: 6 },
  sec_aa: { slot: "secondary", classes: ["dd", "cl", "ca", "bc", "bb", "cv"], m: { aaPct: 60, lightPct: -10 }, costPct: 5 },
  sec_light: { slot: "secondary", classes: ["ca", "bc", "bb"], m: { lightPct: 25, aaPct: -20 }, costPct: 5 },
  torp_none: { slot: "torpedo", classes: ["dd", "cl", "ca"], m: { torpedoPct: -100 }, costPct: -8 },
  torp_heavy: { slot: "torpedo", classes: ["dd", "cl", "ca", "ss"], tech: "torpedo_1", m: { torpedoPct: 40 }, costPct: 8 },
  asw_sonar: { slot: "asw", classes: ["dd", "cl"], tech: "sonar_1", m: { subDetectPct: 30, depthPct: 15 }, costPct: 6 },
  asw_dc_heavy: { slot: "asw", classes: ["dd"], m: { depthPct: 40, torpedoPct: -25 }, costPct: 4 },
  fc_improved: { slot: "fire_control", classes: CAP, tech: "fire_control_1", m: { hitPct: 6 }, costPct: 5 },
  radar_surface: { slot: "fire_control", classes: [...CAP, "cv"], tech: "naval_radar_1", m: { surfDetectPct: 20, hitPct: 5 }, costPct: 6 },
  radar_adv: { slot: "fire_control", classes: [...CAP, "cv"], tech: "naval_radar_2", m: { surfDetectPct: 35, subDetectPct: 10, hitPct: 8 }, costPct: 9 },
  radar_microwave: { slot: "fire_control", classes: [...CAP, "cv", "dd"], project: "sp_cavity_magnetron", m: { surfDetectPct: 50, hitPct: 12, aaPct: 10 }, costPct: 12 },
  armor_heavy: { slot: "armor", classes: CAP, m: { armorPct: 15, hpPct: 5, speedCentiKn: -150 }, costPct: 10 },
  armor_light: { slot: "armor", classes: CAP, m: { armorPct: -15, speedCentiKn: 150 }, costPct: -8 },
  armor_deck: { slot: "armor", classes: ["cv"], tech: "armored_deck", m: { damageTakenPct: -25, deckPct: -15 }, costPct: 12 },
  engine_fast: { slot: "engine", classes: ["ca", "bc", "bb", "cv"], m: { speedCentiKn: 200, rangePct: -10, fuelPct: 15 }, costPct: 8 },
  engine_long: { slot: "engine", classes: ["ca", "bc", "bb", "cv", "ss"], m: { rangePct: 30, speedCentiKn: -100 }, costPct: 4 },
  hangar_large: { slot: "hangar", classes: ["cv"], m: { deckPct: 25, hpPct: -5 }, costPct: 10 },
  hangar_small: { slot: "hangar", classes: ["cv"], m: { deckPct: -25 }, costPct: -15 },
  mine_layer: { slot: "special", classes: ["dd", "cl", "ss"], tech: "mine_warfare_1", m: { torpedoPct: -20 }, costPct: 4, flags: DS_MINE_LAYER },
  minesweep: { slot: "special", classes: ["dd", "cl"], tech: "mine_warfare_1", m: {}, costPct: 3, flags: DS_MINESWEEP },
  floatplane: { slot: "special", classes: CAP, m: { surfDetectPct: 15 }, costPct: 3 },
  snorkel: { slot: "special", classes: ["ss"], tech: "snorkel", m: { visPct: -25, speedCentiKn: 100 }, costPct: 8 },
  shbb_hull: { slot: "special", classes: ["bb"], gens: [3, 4], tech: "super_heavy_battleship",
    m: { hpPct: 40, heavyPct: 30, piercePct: 15, armorPct: 25, speedCentiKn: -400, visPct: 20 }, costPct: 60 },
};
export function shipBase(base: string): { cls: ShipClass; gen: number } | null {
  const [c, g] = base.split("_");
  const cls = c as ShipClass;
  const gen = Number(g);
  return SHIP_CLASSES.includes(cls) && Number.isInteger(gen) && gen >= 1 && gen <= SHIP_GENS[cls] ? { cls, gen } : null;
}
export function shipDesignMods(def: DesignDef): ShipDesignMods {
  const { cls, gen } = shipBase(def.base)!;
  const o: ShipDesignMods = {
    kind: "ship", lightPct: 0, heavyPct: 0, torpedoPct: 0, depthPct: 0, aaPct: 0, armorPct: 0, hpPct: 0, piercePct: 0,
    deckPct: 0, damageTakenPct: 0, surfDetectPct: 0, subDetectPct: 0, visPct: 0, rangePct: 0, fuelPct: 0, hitPct: 0,
    speedCentiKn: 0, costPct: 0, flags: 0, year: shipYear(cls, gen),
  };
  for (const id of def.modules) {
    if (id === "") continue;
    const m = SHIP_MODULE_DEFS[id];
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(m.m)) {
      if (k === "mainPct") { if (cls === "cl") o.lightPct += v; else o.heavyPct += v; }
      else (o as unknown as Record<string, number>)[k] += v;
    }
    o.costPct += m.costPct; o.flags |= m.flags ?? 0;
  }
  return o;
}
/** P4 ShipStats field names (plan decision 1: grep `export interface ShipStats`). */
export interface ShipStatsLike {
  hpMilli: number; light: number; lightPierce: number; heavy: number; heavyPierce: number; torpedo: number; depth: number;
  armor: number; aa: number; speedCentiKn: number; rangeKm: number; vis: number; surfDetect: number; subDetect: number;
  deck: number; fuelPerDayMilli: number; cost: number;
}
export function applyShipDesign<T extends ShipStatsLike>(s: T, m: ShipDesignMods | null): T {
  if (m === null) return s;
  return {
    ...s,
    hpMilli: mul(s.hpMilli, m.hpPct), light: mul(s.light, m.lightPct), heavy: mul(s.heavy, m.heavyPct),
    lightPierce: mul(s.lightPierce, m.piercePct), heavyPierce: mul(s.heavyPierce, m.piercePct),
    torpedo: mul(s.torpedo, m.torpedoPct), depth: mul(s.depth, m.depthPct), armor: mul(s.armor, m.armorPct),
    aa: mul(s.aa, m.aaPct), speedCentiKn: Math.max(100, s.speedCentiKn + m.speedCentiKn), rangeKm: mul(s.rangeKm, m.rangePct),
    vis: mul(s.vis, m.visPct), surfDetect: mul(s.surfDetect, m.surfDetectPct), subDetect: mul(s.subDetect, m.subDetectPct),
    deck: mul(s.deck, m.deckPct), fuelPerDayMilli: mul(s.fuelPerDayMilli, m.fuelPct), cost: mul(s.cost, m.costPct),
  };
}
/** The year of a ship class generation: the year of its P4 tech (gen 1 = 1936). */
export function shipYear(cls: ShipClass, gen: number): number {
  return gen === 1 ? 1936 : WW_TECHS[`${cls}_${gen}` as WwTechId]?.year ?? 1936;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- planes (spec §5.4)
type Size = "small" | "medium" | "large";
interface PlaneBaseDef {
  tech?: WwTechId; project?: SpecialProject; size: Size; agility: number; speed: number; range: number;
  airDef: number; relPm: number; costMilli: number; res: number[];
}
const pb = (tech: WwTechId | null, size: Size, ag: number, sp: number, rg: number, ad: number, rel: number, cost: number,
  r: Partial<Record<ResourceId, number>>, project?: SpecialProject): PlaneBaseDef =>
  ({ ...(tech ? { tech } : {}), ...(project ? { project } : {}), size, agility: ag, speed: sp, range: rg, airDef: ad * 100,
     relPm: rel * 10, costMilli: cost * 1000, res: res(r) });
export const PLANE_BASE_DEFS: Readonly<Record<string, PlaneBaseDef>> = {
  small_1: pb("fighter_1", "small", 50, 450, 650, 10, 80, 16, { aluminium: 2, rubber: 1 }),
  small_2: pb("fighter_2", "small", 55, 530, 780, 13, 82, 20, { aluminium: 2, rubber: 1 }),
  small_3: pb("fighter_3", "small", 60, 610, 910, 16, 85, 24, { aluminium: 2, rubber: 1 }),
  medium_1: pb("bomber_1", "medium", 22, 380, 900, 14, 80, 24, { aluminium: 3, rubber: 1 }),
  medium_2: pb("bomber_2", "medium", 26, 460, 1080, 18, 82, 30, { aluminium: 3, rubber: 1 }),
  medium_3: pb("heavy_fighter_3", "medium", 30, 540, 1260, 22, 85, 36, { aluminium: 3, rubber: 1 }),
  large_1: pb("strat_bomber_1", "large", 10, 350, 1500, 22, 78, 40, { aluminium: 4, rubber: 2 }),
  large_2: pb("strat_bomber_2", "large", 12, 430, 1800, 28, 80, 48, { aluminium: 4, rubber: 2 }),
  large_3: pb("strat_bomber_3", "large", 14, 510, 2100, 34, 82, 56, { aluminium: 4, rubber: 2 }),
  jet_1: pb(null, "small", 45, 820, 450, 14, 65, 30, { aluminium: 3, rubber: 1 }, "sp_jet_engine"),
};
export function planeSlots(base: string): string[] {
  const s = PLANE_BASE_DEFS[base]?.size;
  return s === "large" ? ["weapon", "aux", "aux", "defense", "defense", "special"]
    : s === "medium" ? ["weapon", "aux", "aux", "defense", "special"] : ["weapon", "aux", "aux", "special"];
}
interface PlaneModDef {
  group: "weapon" | "defense" | "special"; sizes?: readonly Size[]; bases?: readonly string[]; tech?: WwTechId;
  air?: number; ground?: number; strat?: number; naval?: number; groundX?: number; stratX?: number; navalX?: number;
  agility?: number; speedPct?: number; rangePct?: number; airDef?: number; relPp?: number; detect?: number;
  costMilli?: number; costPct?: number; flags?: number;
}
const MLG: readonly Size[] = ["medium", "large"];
export const PLANE_MODULE_DEFS: Readonly<Record<string, PlaneModDef>> = {
  w_mg2: { group: "weapon", air: 6, ground: 0.5, costMilli: 500 },
  w_mg4: { group: "weapon", air: 10, ground: 1, costMilli: 1_000 },
  w_hmg: { group: "weapon", bases: ["small_2", "small_3", "jet_1"], air: 13, ground: 1.5, costMilli: 1_500 },
  w_cannon20: { group: "weapon", tech: "aircraft_cannon_1", air: 15, ground: 2, naval: 0.5, agility: -2, costMilli: 2_000 },
  w_cannon30: { group: "weapon", tech: "aircraft_cannon_2", air: 20, ground: 4, naval: 1, agility: -4, costMilli: 3_000 },
  w_bombs_s: { group: "weapon", ground: 6, strat: 1, naval: 2, speedPct: -5, costMilli: 1_000 },
  w_bombs_m: { group: "weapon", sizes: MLG, ground: 8, strat: 5, naval: 3, speedPct: -8, costMilli: 2_000 },
  w_bombs_l: { group: "weapon", sizes: ["large"], ground: 6, strat: 10, naval: 2, speedPct: -10, costMilli: 4_000 },
  w_torpedo: { group: "weapon", tech: "naval_bomber_1", naval: 12, speedPct: -8, costMilli: 2_000 },
  w_rockets: { group: "weapon", tech: "air_rockets_1", ground: 6, naval: 5, costMilli: 2_000 },
  w_cargo: { group: "weapon", sizes: MLG, costMilli: 1_000, flags: DS_CARGO },
  m_def_turret: { group: "defense", sizes: MLG, airDef: 8, speedPct: -5, costMilli: 1_500 },
  m_armor: { group: "defense", airDef: 6, agility: -4, costMilli: 1_000 },
  m_self_sealing: { group: "special", tech: "self_sealing_tanks", airDef: 3, relPp: 5, costMilli: 500 },
  m_drop_tank: { group: "special", tech: "drop_tanks", rangePct: 40, agility: -3, costMilli: 500 },
  m_dive_brakes: { group: "special", sizes: ["small"], groundX: 125, navalX: 125, speedPct: -5, costMilli: 500 },
  m_bomb_sight: { group: "special", sizes: MLG, tech: "bomb_sight_1", groundX: 110, stratX: 120, costMilli: 1_000 },
  m_radar: { group: "special", tech: "air_radar_1", detect: 20, costMilli: 2_000, flags: DS_RADAR },
  m_carrier: { group: "special", sizes: ["small"], tech: "carrier_aviation", rangePct: -10, costMilli: 1_000, flags: DS_CARRIER },
  m_engine_boost: { group: "special", speedPct: 8, rangePct: -10, costPct: 10 },
  m_recon_camera: { group: "special", costMilli: 500, flags: DS_RECON },
};
/** Which module groups a plane slot kind takes (spec §5.4.2 after the table). */
export function planeSlotTakes(slot: string, id: string): boolean {
  const g = PLANE_MODULE_DEFS[id]?.group;
  if (slot === "weapon") return g === "weapon";
  if (slot === "aux") return g !== undefined && id !== "w_cargo";
  if (slot === "defense") return g === "defense";
  return g === "special";
}
export function planeType(def: DesignDef): string {
  const size = PLANE_BASE_DEFS[def.base].size;
  const m = new Set(def.modules.filter((x) => x !== ""));
  const bombs = m.has("w_bombs_s") || m.has("w_bombs_m") || m.has("w_bombs_l");
  const cv = m.has("m_carrier");
  if (m.has("w_cargo")) return "transport_plane";
  if (size === "small") {
    if (m.has("w_torpedo")) return cv ? "cv_naval_bomber" : "naval_bomber";
    if (bombs || m.has("w_rockets") || m.has("m_dive_brakes")) return cv ? "cv_cas" : "cas";
    return cv ? "cv_fighter" : "fighter";
  }
  if (m.has("w_torpedo") || (m.has("m_radar") && !bombs)) return "naval_bomber";
  if (bombs) return size === "medium" ? "bomber" : "strat_bomber";
  return size === "medium" ? "heavy_fighter" : "strat_bomber";
}
export function planeStats(def: DesignDef, ctx: DesignCtx): PlaneDesignStats {
  const b = PLANE_BASE_DEFS[def.base];
  let air = 0, ground = 0, strat = 0, naval = 0, gX = 100, sX = 100, nX = 100, agility = b.agility, speedPct = 0, rangePct = 0;
  let airDef = b.airDef, rel = b.relPm + ctx.relPp * 10, detect = 0, cost = b.costMilli, costPct = 0, flags = b.project ? DS_JET : 0;
  for (const id of def.modules) {
    if (id === "") continue;
    const m = PLANE_MODULE_DEFS[id];
    air += Math.round((m.air ?? 0) * 100); ground += Math.round((m.ground ?? 0) * 100);
    strat += Math.round((m.strat ?? 0) * 100); naval += Math.round((m.naval ?? 0) * 100);
    gX = Math.floor((gX * (m.groundX ?? 100)) / 100); sX = Math.floor((sX * (m.stratX ?? 100)) / 100); nX = Math.floor((nX * (m.navalX ?? 100)) / 100);
    agility += m.agility ?? 0; speedPct += m.speedPct ?? 0; rangePct += m.rangePct ?? 0; airDef += (m.airDef ?? 0) * 100;
    rel += (m.relPp ?? 0) * 10; detect += m.detect ?? 0; cost += m.costMilli ?? 0; costPct += m.costPct ?? 0; flags |= m.flags ?? 0;
  }
  if ((flags & DS_RADAR) !== 0 && (ctx.projects & projectBit("sp_cavity_magnetron")) !== 0) detect = Math.floor((detect * 3) / 2);
  return {
    kind: "plane", type: planeType(def), airAttack: air, airDefense: airDef, agility: Math.max(1, agility),
    ground: Math.floor((ground * gX) / 100), strategic: Math.floor((strat * sX) / 100), naval: Math.floor((naval * nX) / 100),
    speed: mul(b.speed, speedPct), range: mul(b.range, rangePct), detect,
    relPm: clamp(rel, 400, 990), costMilli: mul(mul(cost, costPct), ctx.costPct), resources: [...b.res],
    fuelPct: (flags & DS_JET) !== 0 ? 60 : 0, flags,
    year: b.tech ? WW_TECHS[b.tech].year : 1944,
  };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- shared
export function designStats(def: DesignDef, ctx: DesignCtx): TankDesignStats | ShipDesignMods | PlaneDesignStats {
  return def.kind === "tank" ? tankStats(def, ctx) : def.kind === "ship" ? shipDesignMods(def) : planeStats(def, ctx);
}
export function slotKinds(def: Pick<DesignDef, "kind" | "base">): string[] {
  return def.kind === "tank" ? tankSlots(def.base) : def.kind === "plane" ? planeSlots(def.base)
    : [...(SHIP_CLASS_SLOTS[shipBase(def.base)?.cls ?? "dd"] ?? [])];
}
/** Spec §5.1 refusals, the order of the checks fixed (the screen shows the first). */
export function designBlock(def: DesignDef, c: DesignBlockCtx): WwOrderBlock | null {
  const has = (t?: WwTechId, p?: SpecialProject) => (t === undefined || c.techs.has(t)) && (p === undefined || (c.projects & projectBit(p)) !== 0);
  if (def.kind === "tank") {
    const b = TANK_BASE_DEFS[def.base];
    if (b === undefined || !has(b.tech)) return "bad_base";
    const slots = tankSlots(def.base);
    if (def.modules.length !== slots.length) return "module_bad_slot";
    const m = resolvedModules(def);
    const specials = m.slice(5).filter((x) => x !== "");
    if (new Set(specials).size !== specials.length) return "module_bad_slot";
    for (let i = 0; i < m.length; i++) {
      if (m[i] === "") { if (slots[i] !== "special") return "module_bad_slot"; continue; }
      const d = TANK_MODULE_DEFS[m[i]];
      if (d === undefined || d.slot !== slots[i]) return "module_bad_slot";
      if (d.weights !== undefined && !d.weights.includes(b.weight)) return "module_bad_slot";
      if (d.bases !== undefined && !d.bases.includes(def.base)) return "module_bad_slot";
      if (!has(d.tech)) return "module_locked";
    }
    if (m[0] === "howitzer" && m[1] !== "turret_none" && m[1] !== "turret_open") return "module_bad_slot";
    if (m[0] === "aa_auto" && m[1] !== "turret_open") return "module_bad_slot";
    return null;
  }
  if (def.kind === "ship") {
    const sb = shipBase(def.base);
    if (sb === null || (sb.gen > 1 && !has(`${sb.cls}_${sb.gen}` as WwTechId))) return "bad_base";
    const slots = SHIP_CLASS_SLOTS[sb.cls];
    if (def.modules.length !== slots.length) return "module_bad_slot";
    for (let i = 0; i < slots.length; i++) {
      const id = def.modules[i];
      if (id === "") continue;
      const d = SHIP_MODULE_DEFS[id];
      if (d === undefined || d.slot !== slots[i] || !d.classes.includes(sb.cls)) return "module_bad_slot";
      if (d.gens !== undefined && !d.gens.includes(sb.gen)) return "module_bad_slot";
      if (!has(d.tech, d.project)) return "module_locked";
    }
    if (c.treatyMember && (sb.cls === "bb" || sb.cls === "bc")) {
      if (def.modules.includes("shbb_hull")) return "treaty_limit";
      if (!c.treatyEscalated && def.modules.includes("main_heavy")) return "treaty_limit";
    }
    return null;
  }
  const b = PLANE_BASE_DEFS[def.base];
  if (b === undefined || !has(b.tech, b.project)) return "bad_base";
  const slots = planeSlots(def.base);
  if (def.modules.length !== slots.length || def.modules[0] === "") return "module_bad_slot";
  for (let i = 0; i < slots.length; i++) {
    const id = def.modules[i];
    if (id === "") continue;
    const d = PLANE_MODULE_DEFS[id];
    if (d === undefined || !planeSlotTakes(slots[i], id)) return "module_bad_slot";
    if (d.sizes !== undefined && !d.sizes.includes(b.size)) return "module_bad_slot";
    if (d.bases !== undefined && !d.bases.includes(def.base)) return "module_bad_slot";
    if (!has(d.tech)) return "module_locked";
  }
  return null;
}
const XP_COST: Readonly<Record<DesignKind, readonly [number, number, number]>> = { tank: [10, 3, 1], ship: [15, 5, 0], plane: [10, 3, 0] };
/** Spec §5.1 cost table: a new design, or a copy (changed modules and levels, at least 5). */
export function designXpCost(before: DesignDef | null, after: DesignDef): number {
  const [base, perMod, perLvl] = XP_COST[after.kind];
  const lv = (d: DesignDef) => [d.armorLevel ?? 0, d.engineLevel ?? 0];
  if (before === null || before.base !== after.base || before.kind !== after.kind) {
    const m = resolvedModules(after);
    const nonDefault = m.filter((id, i) => id !== "" && (after.kind !== "tank" || id !== tankDefault(after.base, i))).length;
    return base + perMod * nonDefault + perLvl * (lv(after)[0] + lv(after)[1]);
  }
  const a = resolvedModules(before), b = resolvedModules(after);
  const changed = b.filter((id, i) => id !== a[i]).length;
  const lvl = Math.abs(lv(after)[0] - lv(before)[0]) + Math.abs(lv(after)[1] - lv(before)[1]);
  return Math.max(5, perMod * changed + perLvl * lvl);
}
/** Spec §5.5. */
export function convertCost(newCostMilli: number, oldCostMilli: number): number {
  return Math.max(Math.floor((newCostMilli * 25) / 100), newCostMilli - Math.floor((oldCostMilli * 60) / 100));
}
/** Spec §5.5: retention between two design keys, bp; -1: the P1 rule decides. */
export function designRetentionBp(a: DesignDef | null, b: DesignDef | null): number {
  if (a === null || b === null || a.kind !== b.kind) return -1;
  if (a.base === b.base) return 9_000;
  const ra = a.kind === "tank" ? tankRole(a) : a.kind === "plane" ? planeType(a) : a.base.split("_")[0];
  const rb = b.kind === "tank" ? tankRole(b) : b.kind === "plane" ? planeType(b) : b.base.split("_")[0];
  return ra === rb ? 6_000 : -1;
}
/** Spec §5.6 / §7.1: licence production surcharge, percent. */
export function licenceCostPct(gapYears: number): number {
  return 10 + Math.min(50, 10 * Math.max(0, gapYears));
}
/** Spec §5.2.4: the draw order inside a need group — newest year first, then the smaller key. */
export function drawOrder(keys: readonly number[], yearOf: (key: number) => number): number[] {
  return [...keys].sort((a, b) => yearOf(b) - yearOf(a) || a - b);
}
/** Reliability effect on equipment losses (spec §5.2.2): × (180 − rel%) / 100, as bp. */
export function reliabilityLossBp(relPm: number): number {
  return Math.floor(((1800 - relPm) * 100) / 10);
}
/** Spec §5.2.3: the screen's "recommended fill" and the AI's starting point (K5's AI_DESIGN_PRESETS refines). */
export function recommendedTank(base: string, techs: ReadonlySet<WwTechId>, role: TankRole): DesignDef { /* E fills */ }
```

`DS_AMPHIBIOUS`처럼 표에서만 쓰는 이름 말고 안 쓰는 import는 린트가 잡으면 지운다. `recommendedTank`·`recommendedShip`·`recommendedPlane`은 기반이 시그니처와 `throw`없는 최소 본문(바탕 기본값 + 쓸 수 있는 첫 모듈)만, E가 spec §5.2.3 표대로 채운다.

- [ ] **Step 6: 교리·지휘 규칙 `DoctrineRules.ts`, `CommandRules.ts`** (spec §3, §4 — 숙련 증가·레벨·대교리 표·합산은 **기반 본문**, 세부 교리 보상 표 `SUBDOCTRINE_REWARDS`·장교단 표 `ARMY_SPIRIT_STATS`는 D가 채운다)

```ts
// packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/DoctrineRules.ts
import {
  DOCTRINE_TRACKS, GD_COMPLETE_LEVELS, GD_DOMAIN, GRAND_DOCTRINES, MASTERY_BASE_DAY_MILLI, MASTERY_CAP_DAY_MILLI,
  MASTERY_LEVELS_MILLI, MASTERY_MAX_MILLI, SUB_TRACK, SUBDOCTRINES, Subdoctrine, GrandDoctrine,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/DepthTypes";
import type { WwStats } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/PoliticsTypes";

export function masteryLevel(milli: number): 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 {
  let l = 0;
  while (l < 5 && milli >= MASTERY_LEVELS_MILLI[l]) l++;
  return l as 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5;
}
/** Spec §3.4: combat share per track that day, micro-mastery (plan decision 12). */
export type TrackTally = readonly [number, number, number];
/** Spec §3.4: today's gain for the domain's three tracks, milli. */
export function masteryGain(tally: TrackTally, speedPct: number): [number, number, number] {
  const g = (t: number) =>
    Math.floor((Math.min(MASTERY_CAP_DAY_MILLI, MASTERY_BASE_DAY_MILLI + Math.floor(t / 1000)) * (100 + speedPct)) / 100);
  return [g(tally[0]), g(tally[1]), g(tally[2])];
}
export function addMastery(milli: number, gain: number): number {
  return Math.min(MASTERY_MAX_MILLI, Math.max(0, milli + gain));
}
/** Land combat share of one division (spec §3.4 row 1), micro-mastery per track. Weights in half battalions. */
export function landCombatShare(infHalf: number, armorHalf: number, supportHalf: number): TrackTally {
  const total = infHalf + armorHalf + supportHalf;
  if (total <= 0) return [0, 0, 0];
  return [Math.floor((20_000 * infHalf) / total), Math.floor((20_000 * armorHalf) / total), Math.floor((20_000 * supportHalf) / total)];
}
/** Spec §3.4 rows 2–3, micro-mastery. */
export const NAVAL_SHARE_MICRO = { surfaceBattle: 30_000, surfaceMission: 2_000, escortBattle: 20_000, escortMission: 1_000, carrierBattle: 50_000, carrierMission: 4_000 } as const;
export const AIR_SHARE_MICRO_PER_SORTIE = 500;

export interface DoctrineChoice {
  /** GRAND_DOCTRINES index per domain, -1 none. */
  gd: readonly number[];
  /** SUBDOCTRINES index per track (9), -1 none. */
  sub: readonly number[];
}
/** Spec §3.2: base and completion stats of each grand doctrine (the old doctrine techs' tier 1 / tier 2 values). */
export const GRAND_DOCTRINE_STATS: Readonly<Record<GrandDoctrine, readonly [Partial<WwStats>, Partial<WwStats>]>> = {
  gd_mobile: [{ speedPct: 10 }, { mechBreakPct: 20, mechOrg: 5 }],
  gd_firepower: [{ artillerySoftPct: 20 }, { attackPct: 10 }],
  gd_battleplan: [{ defensePct: 10, planMaxBp: 1_000 }, { defensePct: 10, planSpeedPct: 50, entrenchMax: 2 }],
  gd_mass: [{ recruitablePct: 10, infTrainCutPct: 33 }, { attackPct: 5, defensePct: 5, infOrg: 10 }],
  gd_fleet_in_being: [{ capitalAttackPct: 10, positioningPct: 5 }, { screeningPct: 15, navyDefensePct: 10 }],
  gd_trade_interdiction: [{ subAttackPct: 15, subVisibilityPct: -10, convoyRaidPct: 15 }, { convoyRaidPct: 15, navalOrg: 10 }],
  gd_base_strike: [{ carrierStrikePct: 20, carrierDeckPct: 10 }, { navalStrikePct: 15, carrierCycleCut: 1 }],
  gd_strategic_destruction: [{ stratBombPct: 15, fighterAttackPct: 5 }, { stratBombPct: 15, bomberDefensePct: 15 }],
  gd_battlefield_support: [{ casPct: 15, enemyAirPenaltyPp: 5 }, { casPct: 15, paradropPrepPct: -50 }],
  gd_operational_integrity: [{ fighterAgilityPct: 10, airDetectPct: 10 }, { airLossCutPct: 15, navalStrikePct: 10 }],
};
/** Spec §3.3 L1..L5 of every sub-doctrine (D fills from the spec tables; key choice per Task 2 Step 1). */
export const SUBDOCTRINE_REWARDS: Readonly<Record<Subdoctrine, readonly Partial<WwStats>[]>> =
  Object.fromEntries(SUBDOCTRINES.map((s) => [s, [{}, {}, {}, {}, {}]])) as Record<Subdoctrine, Partial<WwStats>[]>;
/** Spec §3.2–§3.3: the doctrine part of a country's stats. `levels`: mastery level per track (9). */
export function doctrineStats(choice: DoctrineChoice, levels: readonly number[]): Partial<WwStats> {
  const out: Record<string, number> = {};
  const addIn = (s: Partial<WwStats>) => { for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(s)) out[k] = (out[k] ?? 0) + (v as number); };
  for (let dom = 0; dom < 3; dom++) {
    const g = choice.gd[dom];
    if (g < 0) continue;   // spec §3.2 last bullet: mastery grows but gives nothing without a grand doctrine
    const [base, complete] = GRAND_DOCTRINE_STATS[GRAND_DOCTRINES[g]];
    addIn(base);
    if (levels[dom * 3] + levels[dom * 3 + 1] + levels[dom * 3 + 2] >= GD_COMPLETE_LEVELS) addIn(complete);
    for (let t = dom * 3; t < dom * 3 + 3; t++) {
      const s = choice.sub[t];
      if (s < 0) continue;
      const rewards = SUBDOCTRINE_REWARDS[SUBDOCTRINES[s]];
      for (let l = 0; l < levels[t]; l++) addIn(rewards[l]);
    }
  }
  return out as Partial<WwStats>;
}
/** Refusal of doctrine_pick / subdoctrine_pick (plan decision 40). */
export interface DoctrinePickInput {
  domain: number; gd: number; current: number; lockUntil: number; day: number; xp: number;
  /** For a sub-doctrine: its track and the track's current pick. */
  track?: number; sub?: number; currentSub?: number;
}
export function doctrinePickBlock(i: DoctrinePickInput): "doctrine_locked" | "no_xp" | "bad_index" | null {
  if (i.track !== undefined) {
    if (i.sub === undefined || SUB_TRACK[i.sub] !== i.track) return "bad_index";
    if (i.currentSub === i.sub) return "bad_index";
    if (i.currentSub !== undefined && i.currentSub >= 0 && i.lockUntil > i.day) return "doctrine_locked";
    return i.currentSub !== undefined && i.currentSub >= 0 && i.xp < 25 ? "no_xp" : null;
  }
  if (GD_DOMAIN[i.gd] !== i.domain || i.current === i.gd) return "bad_index";
  if (i.current >= 0 && i.lockUntil > i.day) return "doctrine_locked";
  return i.current >= 0 && i.xp < [100, 75, 75][i.domain] ? "no_xp" : null;
}
export const TRACK_DOMAIN = (t: number) => Math.floor(t / 3);
export { DOCTRINE_TRACKS };
```

`"no_xp"`는 결정 1의 P2 거절 사유(없으면 Step 2 끝 줄대로 기반이 더한 것). 경험치 단위는 P2·P4의 경험치 milli 칸이고 비교는 정수 경험치(× 1000).

```ts
// packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/CommandRules.ts
import { CP_DAY_MILLI, CP_FM_UPKEEP_MILLI, FM_TRAITS, WW_MAX_FIELD_MARSHALS } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/DepthTypes";
import type { GeneralTrait } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/LandTypes";
import { TRAIT_SLOTS } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/LandTypes";

/** Spec §4.2 table: the 12 new general traits. kind "terrain" ones come from 60 battle days in `terrain`. */
export interface GeneralTraitDef {
  kind: "terrain" | "assign"; level: number; needs?: GeneralTrait; cp: number;
  /** P2 terrainDays index (plan: entries 4.. are forest+jungle, urban, marsh). */
  terrainIdx?: number;
}
export const NEW_GENERAL_TRAITS: Readonly<Partial<Record<GeneralTrait, GeneralTraitDef>>> = {
  forest_expert: { kind: "terrain", level: 1, cp: 0, terrainIdx: 4 },
  urban_expert: { kind: "terrain", level: 1, cp: 0, terrainIdx: 5 },
  swamp_expert: { kind: "terrain", level: 1, cp: 0, terrainIdx: 6 },
  infantry_expert: { kind: "assign", level: 2, needs: "infantry_officer", cp: 15 },
  armor_expert: { kind: "assign", level: 2, needs: "panzer_leader", cp: 20 },
  artillery_officer: { kind: "assign", level: 1, cp: 15 },
  engineer_officer: { kind: "assign", level: 2, cp: 15 },
  trickster: { kind: "assign", level: 3, cp: 20 },
  commando: { kind: "assign", level: 2, cp: 15 },
  guerrilla_fighter: { kind: "assign", level: 2, cp: 15 },
  skirmisher: { kind: "assign", level: 1, cp: 15 },
  ambusher: { kind: "assign", level: 3, needs: "defensive_doctrine", cp: 25 },
};
export const TERRAIN_TRAIT_DAYS = 60;
export const FM_EXTRA_TRAIT_CP = 20;
export const FM_TRAIT_LEVELS: readonly number[] = [3, 5];
export const ADMIRAL_ASSIGN_CP = 15;
export const ADMIRAL_ASSIGN_LEVEL = 2;
export function traitSlots(level: number): number {
  return TRAIT_SLOTS[Math.max(1, Math.min(5, level))];
}
/** Spec §4.3: field marshals a country may have. */
export function fieldMarshalCap(divisions: number): number {
  return Math.min(WW_MAX_FIELD_MARSHALS, 1 + Math.floor(divisions / 72));
}
/** Spec §4.1: a day's command power, milli (can be negative with upkeep). */
export function cpDayMilli(cpGainPct: number, fieldMarshals: number): number {
  return Math.floor((CP_DAY_MILLI * (100 + cpGainPct)) / 100) - CP_FM_UPKEEP_MILLI * fieldMarshals;
}
/** Spec §4.3 / plan decision 14: a field marshal's share in bp of a division modifier. */
export function fieldMarshalBp(att: number, dfn: number): { attackBp: number; defenseBp: number } {
  return { attackBp: att * 250, defenseBp: dfn * 250 };
}
/** Spec §4.2 FM traits (whole army group). planMaxBp etc. use the P2 stat names. */
export const FM_TRAIT_STATS: Readonly<Record<(typeof FM_TRAITS)[number], { attackBp?: number; defenseBp?: number; planSpeedPct?: number; planMaxBp?: number; entrenchSpeedPct?: number; supplyUsePct?: number; recoveryPct?: number; org?: number; groupArmies?: number }>> = {
  fm_offensive: { attackBp: 500, planSpeedPct: 10 },
  fm_defensive: { defenseBp: 500, entrenchSpeedPct: 25 },
  fm_logistics: { supplyUsePct: -10 },
  fm_thorough: { planMaxBp: 1_000 },
  fm_fast_planner: { planSpeedPct: 20 },
  fm_inspiring: { recoveryPct: 10, org: 3 },
  fm_strategist: { attackBp: 300, defenseBp: 300, groupArmies: 1 },
};
export interface TraitAssignInput {
  admiral: boolean; level: number; traits: readonly string[]; slots: number; trait: string; cpMilli: number; isFm: boolean; fmTraits: readonly string[];
}
export function traitAssignBlock(i: TraitAssignInput): "no_cp" | "trait_unavailable" | null { /* D fills (spec §4.2) */ return "trait_unavailable"; }
export const MEDAL_STATS = { medal_valor: { attackBp: 300 }, medal_defense: { defenseBp: 300 }, medal_service: { recoveryPct: 10 } } as const;
```

- [ ] **Step 7: 정보 규칙 `packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/IntelRules.ts`** (spec §8 — **전부 기반 본문**; I는 엔진 쪽만)

```ts
import {
  AGENCY_UPGRADES, AgencyUpgrade, ID_AIR, ID_ARMY, ID_CIVIL, INTEL_BAND_FLOORS, INTEL_OPERATIONS, IntelOperation,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/DepthTypes";
import type { WwTechId } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/EconomyTypes";

export interface IntelInput {
  domain: number;
  sameFaction: boolean; neighbour: boolean; atWar: boolean;
  /** max(own network, faction-shared network × 50%) on the target, milli‰ (0..1_000_000). */
  networkMilliPm: number;
  /** The observer has the domain's department upgrade (civil: ag_civil_dept …). */
  department: boolean;
  infiltrated: boolean; decrypted: boolean; exploiting: boolean;
  /** Army only: ≥ 20 own-side recon sorties today over a land zone holding a target region; a skirmisher in contact. */
  recon: boolean; skirmisher: boolean;
  /** Air only: own radar range reaches a target region. */
  radar: boolean;
  targetCounterMissions: number;
  intelPct: number;
}
/** Spec §8.8, the order of the lines fixed. */
export function intelLevel(i: IntelInput): number {
  let v = 5;
  if (i.neighbour) v += 10;
  if (i.sameFaction) v += 10;
  if (i.atWar) v += 15;
  v += Math.min(50, Math.floor(i.networkMilliPm / 20_000));
  if (i.department) v += 10;
  if (i.infiltrated && i.domain !== ID_CIVIL) v += 30;
  if (i.exploiting) v += i.domain === ID_CIVIL ? 15 : 30;
  else if (i.decrypted) v += 10;
  if (i.domain === ID_ARMY) { if (i.recon) v += 20; if (i.skirmisher) v += 20; }
  if (i.domain === ID_AIR && i.radar) v += 10;
  v -= Math.min(30, 10 * i.targetCounterMissions);
  v += Math.floor(i.intelPct / 10);
  v = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, v));
  return i.sameFaction ? Math.max(80, v) : v;
}
export function intelBand(level: number): 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 {
  return level >= INTEL_BAND_FLOORS[3] ? 3 : level >= INTEL_BAND_FLOORS[2] ? 2 : level >= INTEL_BAND_FLOORS[1] ? 1 : 0;
}
/** Spec §8.7. `cryptoTechs` = cryptography techs held, `computingTechs` = computing techs held. */
export function cipherStrength(cryptoTechs: number, computingTechs: number, agCipher: boolean, encryptPct: number): number {
  return 100 + 40 * cryptoTechs + 25 * computingTechs + (agCipher ? 50 : 0) + encryptPct;
}
export function decryptPower(agency: boolean, crypto1: boolean, crypto2: boolean, cryptoTechs: number, computingTechs: number, decryptPct: number): number {
  if (!agency) return 0;
  return 10 + (crypto1 ? 20 : 0) + (crypto2 ? 30 : 0) + 15 * cryptoTechs + 10 * computingTechs + decryptPct;
}
/** Spec §8.7: milli‰ a day (Britain → Germany 1939: D 100, E 255 → 1633). */
export function decryptPerDayMilli(d: number, e: number): number {
  return d <= 0 || e <= 0 ? 0 : Math.floor((d * 1_000_000) / (e * 240));
}
export const CRYPTO_TECHS: readonly WwTechId[] = ["cryptography_1", "cryptography_2", "cryptography_3"];
export const COMPUTING_TECHS: readonly WwTechId[] = ["computing_1", "computing_2"];
/** Plan decision 18: the target's counter-intelligence in half units. */
export function counterHalf(counterMissions: number, agCounter: boolean): number {
  return counterMissions + (agCounter ? 2 : 0);
}
/** Plan decision 18: network growth of one build_network operative a day, milli‰. */
export function networkGainMilli(skill: number, intelPct: number, targetCounterMissions: number, targetAgCounter: boolean): number {
  const g = Math.floor((3000 * (1 + skill) * (100 + intelPct)) / 200);
  const cut = Math.min(90, Math.min(75, 25 * targetCounterMissions) + (targetAgCounter ? 30 : 0));
  return Math.floor((g * (100 - cut)) / 100);
}
export const NETWORK_DECAY_MILLI = 1_000;
/** Spec §8.3: capture chance today, milli‰ (compare wwRoll % 1_000_000). */
export function capturePmMilli(onOperation: boolean, skill: number, counterHalfUnits: number, exileOwnCore: boolean): number {
  const base = onOperation ? 5 : 2;
  const p = Math.floor((base * 1000 * (2 + counterHalfUnits)) / 2);
  const q = Math.floor((p * (100 - 25 * (skill - 1))) / 100);
  return exileOwnCore ? Math.floor(q / 2) : q;
}
/** Spec §8.5 table. */
export interface OperationDef { networkPm: number; operatives: 1 | 2; days: number; pp: number; infEquipment: number; rolls: boolean }
export const OPERATION_DEFS: Readonly<Record<IntelOperation, OperationDef>> = {
  op_infiltrate_army: { networkPm: 300, operatives: 1, days: 60, pp: 25, infEquipment: 0, rolls: false },
  op_infiltrate_navy: { networkPm: 300, operatives: 1, days: 60, pp: 25, infEquipment: 0, rolls: false },
  op_infiltrate_air: { networkPm: 300, operatives: 1, days: 60, pp: 25, infEquipment: 0, rolls: false },
  op_steal_blueprints: { networkPm: 400, operatives: 1, days: 90, pp: 25, infEquipment: 0, rolls: false },
  op_capture_cipher: { networkPm: 500, operatives: 2, days: 90, pp: 50, infEquipment: 0, rolls: false },
  op_sabotage: { networkPm: 300, operatives: 1, days: 45, pp: 25, infEquipment: 0, rolls: false },
  op_collaboration: { networkPm: 600, operatives: 2, days: 120, pp: 50, infEquipment: 0, rolls: true },
  op_coup: { networkPm: 800, operatives: 2, days: 150, pp: 100, infEquipment: 1_000, rolls: true },
  op_rescue: { networkPm: 300, operatives: 1, days: 30, pp: 25, infEquipment: 0, rolls: false },
  op_uprising: { networkPm: 400, operatives: 1, days: 60, pp: 25, infEquipment: 500, rolls: false },
};
/** Spec §8.5: days after ag_army_dept (−25% for the army infiltration), ag_special_ops (−20%), opSpeedPct. */
export function operationDays(op: IntelOperation, armyDept: boolean, specialOps: boolean, opSpeedPct: number): number {
  let cut = (specialOps ? 20 : 0) + (armyDept && op === "op_infiltrate_army" ? 25 : 0) + opSpeedPct;
  cut = Math.min(80, Math.max(-100, cut));
  return Math.max(1, Math.floor((OPERATION_DEFS[op].days * (100 - cut)) / 100));
}
/** Spec §8.5: success ‰ of op_collaboration / op_coup (deterministic threshold for wwRoll % 1000). */
export function opSuccessPm(networkPm: number, avgSkillMilli: number, civilDept: boolean, targetCounterHalf: number): number {
  const v = 400 + Math.floor(networkPm / 2) + Math.floor((100 * (avgSkillMilli - 1000)) / 1000) + (civilDept ? 50 : 0) - 50 * targetCounterHalf;
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(950, v));
}
/** Spec §8.5 op_coup: the needed share of my ideology in the target, bp. */
export function coupNeedBp(targetStabilityBp: number): number {
  return targetStabilityBp < 5_000 ? 3_000 : 4_000;
}
/** Spec §8.2: upgrade prerequisites (computing_1 for ag_crypto_2 is checked by the caller). */
export const UPGRADE_NEEDS: Readonly<Partial<Record<AgencyUpgrade, AgencyUpgrade>>> = {
  ag_ops_2: "ag_ops_1", ag_ops_3: "ag_ops_2", ag_crypto_2: "ag_crypto_1", ag_special_ops: "ag_ops_1",
};
export const upgradeBit = (u: AgencyUpgrade) => 1 << AGENCY_UPGRADES.indexOf(u);
export function operativeSlots(upgrades: number): number {
  return 1 + ["ag_ops_1", "ag_ops_2", "ag_ops_3"].filter((u) => (upgrades & upgradeBit(u as AgencyUpgrade)) !== 0).length;
}
/** Spec §8.9: the division-count label of an enemy army token. band 0 "?", 1 ±50% (2-rounded), 2 ±20%, 3 exact. */
export function fogCountLabel(count: number, band: number): string {
  if (band <= 0) return "?";
  if (band >= 3) return String(count);
  const pct = band === 1 ? 50 : 20;
  const step = band === 1 ? 2 : 1;
  const lo = Math.max(0, Math.floor((count * (100 - pct)) / 100 / step) * step);
  const hi = Math.ceil((count * (100 + pct)) / 100 / step) * step;
  return lo === hi ? String(lo) : `${lo}~${hi}`;
}
/**
 * Spec §8.9 attack-preview row. The middle of a symmetric range is the true value and would leak it,
 * so below "exact" the preview uses the top of the band's range (never optimistic for the attacker).
 */
export function fogPreviewValue(count: number, band: number): number {
  if (band >= 3) return count;
  const pct = band <= 0 ? 100 : band === 1 ? 50 : 20;
  return Math.ceil((count * (100 + pct)) / 100);
}
export interface OpStartInput {
  agency: boolean; op: IntelOperation; networkPm: number; operativesFree: number; pp: number; infEquipment: number;
  atWar: boolean; targetHasCaptive: boolean; targetControlsMyCore: boolean; ideologyBp: number; targetStabilityBp: number; halfThreshold: boolean;
}
export function opStartBlock(i: OpStartInput): "no_agency" | "network_low" | "no_operative" | "op_unavailable" | "no_pp" | null { /* I fills (spec §8.5 conditions) */ return "op_unavailable"; }
```

`fogCountLabel`의 확인(spec §20.7 표, `Fog.test` 줄): 12사단 → 띠 0 `"?"`, 띠 1 `"6~18"`(±50%를 2단위로: 6, 18), 띠 2 `"9~15"`(12 × 0.8 = 9.6 → 9, 14.4 → 15), 띠 3 `"12"`. spec 예의 "8~16"(띠 1, 12) 줄은 ±50%가 아니라 ±33%에 맞춘 글이라 이 함수의 값(6~18)이 기준이다 *(결정: 식이 글보다 앞선다, spec §8.9 "범위 ±50%")*. spec §8.9의 "공격 미리보기는 적 합을 띠의 가운데 값으로"는 가운데 = 참값이라 숨김이 되지 않으므로 `fogPreviewValue`(범위의 위쪽 끝, "모름"은 2배)로 대신한다 *(결정)*.

```ts
// packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/ProjectRules.ts (S fills the …Block bodies)
export interface ProjectDef { facility: FacilityId; days: number; techs: readonly WwTechId[]; after?: SpecialProject; spec: ScientistSpec; uraniumDay: number }
export const PROJECT_DEFS: Readonly<Record<SpecialProject, ProjectDef>> = {
  sp_atomic: { facility: "facility_nuclear", days: 1_900, techs: ["nuclear_physics"], spec: "nuclear", uraniumDay: 2 },
  sp_flying_bomb: { facility: "facility_rocket", days: 500, techs: ["rocketry_1"], spec: "rocket", uraniumDay: 0 },
  sp_ballistic: { facility: "facility_rocket", days: 700, techs: [], after: "sp_flying_bomb", spec: "rocket", uraniumDay: 0 },
  sp_jet_engine: { facility: "facility_science", days: 650, techs: ["fighter_2"], spec: "aviation", uraniumDay: 0 },
  sp_cavity_magnetron: { facility: "facility_science", days: 450, techs: ["radar_1"], spec: "electronics", uraniumDay: 0 },
  sp_proximity_fuze: { facility: "facility_science", days: 500, techs: ["anti_air_2", "electronics_1"], spec: "electronics", uraniumDay: 0 },
};
/** Spec §9.2. */
export function scientistPct(skill: number, matches: boolean): number {
  return 15 * (skill - 1) + (matches ? 25 : 0);
}
/** Spec §9.3: a day's progress, milli‰. Skill 4 + match (+70), full input: 1900 days → 894 a day → 1,119 days. */
export function projectDayMilli(baseDays: number, pct: number, inputPm: number, hasScientist: boolean): number {
  const v = Math.floor((Math.floor((1_000_000 * (100 + pct)) / baseDays) * inputPm) / 100_000);
  return hasScientist ? v : Math.floor(v / 2);
}
export function inputPm(got: number, need: number): number {
  return need <= 0 ? 1000 : Math.min(1000, Math.floor((got * 1000) / need));
}
/** Spec §9.4: one facility's bomb progress a day, milli‰ (8,333 at full uranium). */
export function atomicBombDayMilli(uraniumPm: number): number {
  return Math.floor((Math.floor(1_000_000 / 120) * uraniumPm) / 1000);
}
/** The milestone index (0..2) first reached when progress goes from `before` to `after` ‰, or -1. */
export function milestoneCrossed(beforePm: number, afterPm: number): number {
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) if (beforePm < [300, 600, 900][i] && afterPm >= [300, 600, 900][i]) return i;
  return -1;
}
/** Spec §9.5 interception ‰ by the enemy's air superiority ‰ in that zone. */
export function nukeInterceptPm(enemySupPm: number): number {
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(350, Math.floor((enemySupPm - 300) / 2)));
}
/** Spec §9.5 effect numbers. `nukedBefore`: bombs that hit this country before. */
export interface NukeOutcome {
  destroy: { civ: number; mil: number; dock: number };
  damageMilliPerBuilding: number; divisionLossPct: number; neighbourLossPct: number; portShipHpPct: number;
  warSupportBp: number; surrenderCutPp: number; alliesWarSupportBp: number; tensionPct: number;
  nukedDays: number; buildPct: number; supplyPct: number; resistancePp: number;
}
export function nukeOutcome(civ: number, mil: number, dock: number, nukedBefore: number): NukeOutcome {
  return {
    destroy: { civ: Math.floor(civ / 4), mil: Math.floor(mil / 4), dock: Math.floor(dock / 4) },
    damageMilliPerBuilding: 800, divisionLossPct: 60, neighbourLossPct: 15, portShipHpPct: 50,
    warSupportBp: -1_500, surrenderCutPp: -Math.min(30, 10 * (nukedBefore + 1)) + Math.min(30, 10 * nukedBefore),
    alliesWarSupportBp: -500, tensionPct: 10, nukedDays: 365, buildPct: -50, supplyPct: -50, resistancePp: 10,
  };
}
/** Spec §9.7: flying bombs that get through, with the milli remainder carried in state. */
export function rocketHits(kind: 0 | 1, count: number, enemySupPm: number, aaLevel: number, restMilli: number): { hits: number; restMilli: number } {
  if (kind === 1) return { hits: count, restMilli };
  const through = 1000 - Math.min(600, Math.floor((enemySupPm * 4) / 10) + aaLevel * 40);
  const m = count * through + restMilli;
  return { hits: Math.floor(m / 1000), restMilli: m % 1000 };
}
/** Spec §10 success ‰ (clamped 50..950). */
export function raidSuccessPm(kind: 0 | 1 | 2, i: { expLevel: number; aa: number; supPm: number; enemyDivision: boolean; coastalFort: number; bombers: number }): number {
  const v = kind === 0 ? 600 + 50 * i.expLevel - 60 * i.aa - Math.floor(Math.max(0, i.supPm - 500) / 2) - (i.enemyDivision ? 200 : 0)
    : kind === 1 ? 650 - 80 * i.coastalFort - (i.enemyDivision ? 200 : 0) + 50 * i.expLevel
    : 550 + Math.floor(i.bombers / 10) - 50 * i.aa - Math.floor(Math.max(0, i.supPm - 400) / 2);
  return Math.max(50, Math.min(950, v));
}
export const RAID_TARGETS: Readonly<Record<RaidKind, readonly BuildingId[]>> = {
  raid_para: ["facility_nuclear", "facility_rocket", "facility_science", "air_base", "radar", "dam", "coastal_fort", "rocket_site"],
  raid_commando: ["naval_base", "coastal_fort", "dock", "facility_nuclear", "facility_rocket", "facility_science", "dam"],
  raid_bomb: ["facility_nuclear", "facility_rocket", "facility_science", "air_base", "radar", "dam", "coastal_fort", "rocket_site", "naval_base", "dock", "mil", "civ"],
};
export interface ProjectStartInput { project: SpecialProject; facilityKind: FacilityId | null; facilityBusy: boolean; techs: ReadonlySet<WwTechId>; done: number; running: boolean; nukesOn: boolean }
export function projectStartBlock(i: ProjectStartInput): "no_facility" | "project_locked" | "in_progress" | null { /* S */ return "project_locked"; }
export interface NukeDropInput { stock: number; atWar: boolean; bombersInZone: number; nukesOn: boolean; rocket: boolean; ballisticDone: boolean; rocketKm: number; ballisticStock: number }
export function nukeDropBlock(i: NukeDropInput): "no_atomic" | "nuke_conditions" | "out_of_rocket_range" | null { /* S */ return "nuke_conditions"; }
export interface RaidInput { kind: RaidKind; unitOk: boolean; targetOk: boolean; reachOk: boolean; running: number; intelClose: boolean; facilityTarget: boolean }
export function raidBlock(i: RaidInput): "raid_conditions" | null { /* S */ return "raid_conditions"; }

// packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/MioRules.ts (M polishes; tables final)
export function mioSize(fundsMilli: number): number {
  let n = 0;
  while (n < MIO_MAX_SIZE && 300_000 * (n + 1) * (n + 2) <= fundsMilli) n++;
  return n;
}
export function mioNextMilli(size: number): number {
  return size >= MIO_MAX_SIZE ? 0 : 300_000 * (size + 1) * (size + 2);
}
const popcount = (m: number) => { let c = 0; for (let x = m; x !== 0; x &= x - 1) c++; return c; };
/** Spec §6.2: points left = size − traits bought (the archetype's free trait is not bought). */
export function mioPoints(size: number, traits: number, archetype: number): number {
  return size - (popcount(traits) - ((traits & (1 << MIO_FREE_TRAIT[archetype])) !== 0 ? 1 : 0));
}
/** Spec §6.3: row 2 needs row 1 of its column, row 3 needs row 2; p3 and q3 exclude each other. */
export function mioTraitBlock(traits: number, trait: number, points: number): "bad_index" | "prereq" | "no_slot" | null {
  if ((traits & (1 << trait)) !== 0) return "bad_index";
  const col = Math.floor(trait / 3), row = trait % 3;
  if (row > 0 && (traits & (1 << (col * 3 + row - 1))) === 0) return "prereq";
  if ((trait === 2 && (traits & (1 << 5)) !== 0) || (trait === 5 && (traits & (1 << 2)) !== 0)) return "prereq";
  return points <= 0 ? "no_slot" : null;
}
/** Families whose equipment has reliability (armour, aircraft): q1 is +5pp there, cost −3% elsewhere. */
export const MIO_HAS_REL: readonly boolean[] = [false, false, true, true, true, false];
const F = ["infantry", "artillery", "armor", "air_light", "air_heavy", "naval"] as const;
/** Spec §6.3 tables as stats of the active archetype's traits. */
export function mioStats(family: number, traits: number): Partial<WwStats> {
  const f = F[family];
  const o: Record<string, number> = {};
  const add = (k: string, v: number) => { o[k] = (o[k] ?? 0) + v; };
  const on = (i: number) => (traits & (1 << i)) !== 0;
  if (on(0)) add(`mioCostPct_${f}`, -5);
  if (on(1)) add(`mioEffCapBp_${f}`, 500);
  if (on(2)) { add(`mioCostPct_${f}`, -10); add(`mioRelPp_${f}`, -5); }
  if (on(3)) { if (MIO_HAS_REL[family]) add(`mioRelPp_${f}`, 5); else add(`mioCostPct_${f}`, -3); }
  if (on(4)) add(`mioStatPct_${f}`, 5);
  if (on(5)) { add(`mioStatPct_${f}`, 8); add(`mioCostPct_${f}`, 5); }
  const S: readonly (readonly [string, number][])[][] = [
    [[["infAttackPct", 3]], [["supplyUsePct", -5]], [["motSpeedPct", 5], ["fuelUsePct", -2]]],
    [[["artillerySoftPct", 5]], [["artSpeedPct", 10]], [["atPiercePct", 10]]],
    [[["armorBrkPct", 5]], [["armorArmorPct", 5]], [["armorPiercePct", 8]]],
    [[["fighterAgilityPct", 5], ["airAttackPct", 0]], [["fighterAttackPct", 5]], [["airRangePct", 10], ["carrierDeckPct", 5]]],
    [[["airRangePct", 15]], [["stratBombPct", 8], ["casPct", 8]], [["bomberDefensePct", 8]]],
    [[["navyDefensePct", 5]], [["navyAttackPct", 5]], [["subAttackPct", 10], ["subVisibilityPct", -5]]],
  ];
  for (let r = 0; r < 3; r++) if (on(6 + r)) for (const [k, v] of S[family][r]) add(k, v);
  return o as Partial<WwStats>;
}
/** The MIO family of an equipment type (spec §6.1 table), -1 none. Ship classes and convoys → naval. */
export function mioFamilyOf(type: string, planeDesignType?: string): number { /* M fills from the spec table */ return -1; }

// packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/MarketRules.ts (M fills the …Block bodies)
export function marketDiscountPct(yearsOld: number): number {
  return yearsOld <= 1 ? 100 : yearsOld <= 3 ? 75 : 50;
}
export function marketPriceMilli(costMilli: number, yearsOld: number, count: number): number {
  return Math.floor((costMilli * count * marketDiscountPct(yearsOld)) / 100);
}
/** Spec §7.2: civilian factories handed over for 30 days (1 factory = 5 construction a day). */
export function marketCivs(priceMilli: number): number {
  return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(priceMilli / 150_000));
}
export interface MarketBuyInput { listingExists: boolean; own: boolean; count: number; available: number; atWar: boolean; embargoed: boolean; route: boolean; freeCivs: number; civs: number }
export function marketBuyBlock(i: MarketBuyInput): "listing_bad" | "embargoed" | "no_partner" | "no_civs" | null { /* M */ return "listing_bad"; }
export interface LicenceInput { atWar: boolean; embargoed: boolean; senderHas: boolean; exists: boolean; count: number; obsolete: boolean; transferable: boolean }
export function licenceBlock(i: LicenceInput): "licence_exists" | "embargoed" | "no_partner" | "lines_full" | null { /* M */ return "no_partner"; }

// packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/EnergyRules.ts (final)
export function energySupply(coal: number, hydro: number, plants: number, dams: number, extra: number): number {
  return coal * ENERGY_PER_COAL + hydro * ENERGY_PER_HYDRO + plants * ENERGY_PER_PLANT + dams + extra;
}
export function energyRatioPm(demand: number, supply: number): number {
  return demand <= 0 ? 1000 : Math.min(1000, Math.floor((supply * 1000) / demand));
}
/** Spec §16.2: output and construction percent the next day (0 or −1..−50). */
export function energyPenaltyPct(ratioPm: number): number {
  return ratioPm >= 1000 ? 0 : -Math.min(50, Math.floor((1000 - ratioPm) / 20));
}
/** Spec §16.2: coal the energy wants (rounded up), after the refineries' 2 each. */
export function coalWanted(demand: number, hydro: number, plants: number, dams: number, extra: number, refineries: number): number {
  const gap = Math.max(0, demand - energySupply(0, hydro, plants, dams, extra));
  return refineries * COAL_PER_REFINERY + Math.ceil(gap / ENERGY_PER_COAL);
}

/** Spec §16.6: the harbour's hub level in milli after `days` days (5 → 0 over days 61..120). */
export function harborLevelMilli(days: number): number {
  return days < 60 ? 5_000 : Math.max(0, 5_000 - Math.floor(((days - 60) * 5_000) / 60));
}
export function railGunHitsCenti(guns: number, railLevel: number): number {
  return railLevel >= 2 ? RAILGUN_HIT_CENTI * guns : 0;
}
export function railGunFortCut(guns: number, railLevel: number): number {
  return railLevel >= 2 ? Math.min(2, guns) : 0;
}

/** Mines are milli-units (0..1_000_000 = spec 0..1000). */
export const MINE_LAY_PER_DAY: Readonly<Partial<Record<ShipClass, number>>> = { dd: 2_000, cl: 3_000, ss: 3_000 };
export const MINE_SWEEP_PER_DAY: Readonly<Partial<Record<ShipClass, number>>> = { dd: 3_000, cl: 2_000 };
export function mineDecay(m: number): number { return Math.floor((m * 5) / 1000); }
/** Daily HP damage to a side's ships in the region, milli-HP, before the visibility split. */
export function mineDamageMilli(totalHpMilli: number, enemyMinesMilli: number, sweepers: boolean): number {
  const d = Math.floor((Math.floor(totalHpMilli / 1000) * Math.floor(enemyMinesMilli / 1000) * 4) / 1000);
  return sweepers ? Math.floor(d / 2) : d;
}
export function mineConvoyLossPm(enemyMinesMilli: number): number { return Math.floor(enemyMinesMilli / 10_000); }
export function mineInvasionCutPct(enemyMinesMilli: number): number { return Math.min(10, Math.floor(enemyMinesMilli / 100_000)); }
export const NIGHT_LOSS_BP = 5_000;
export function nightBombBp(nightBombStatBp: number): number { return 6_000 + nightBombStatBp; }

// packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/MechRules.ts (W3 fills the …Block bodies; tables final)
export const CONGRESS = { declare: 6_000, lendLease: 5_000, laws: 5_000, faction: 7_000, stepBp: 20, attackedDays: 90, pearlDays: 365 } as const;
export function congressTargetBp(tensionBp: number, attacked: boolean): number {
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(10_000, 2_000 + Math.floor(tensionBp / 2) + (attacked ? 4_000 : 0)));
}
export function congressStep(cur: number, target: number): number {
  return cur < target ? Math.min(target, cur + CONGRESS.stepBp) : Math.max(target, cur - CONGRESS.stepBp);
}
export function bopBand(valuePm: number): 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 {
  return valuePm <= -600 ? 0 : valuePm < -200 ? 1 : valuePm <= 200 ? 2 : valuePm < 600 ? 3 : 4;
}

export function hexOf(b: Uint8Array): string {
  let s = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) s += (b[i] < 16 ? "0" : "") + b[i].toString(16);
  return s;
}
export function bytesOfHex(s: string, out: Uint8Array): void {
  for (let i = 0; i < out.length && 2 * i + 1 < s.length; i++) out[i] = parseInt(s.substr(2 * i, 2), 16);
}
/** A plan-decision-30 helper for tests: the first n in [0, 100000) whose roll passes. */
export function firstPassing(seed: number, purpose: number, pred: (n: number, roll: number) => boolean, a = 0, b = 0): number { /* body: loop */ }
```

`idHash`·`wwRoll`·`WwStats`·`BuildingId`·`ShipClass` 등 import는 그 파일이 쓰는 것만. `firstPassing`의 본문은 `for (let n = 0; n < 100_000; n++) if (pred(n, wwRoll(seed, purpose, n, a, b))) return n; return -1;`.
